## 任务：Falcon3 GSM8K Eval 准备（代码就绪，不启动）

### 目标
准备 Falcon3 d000 和 d100 的 GSM8K eval 脚本/命令，S4 signals 完成后立即在 cqa1 上接力启动。

### 步骤

1. **SSH 到 cqa1-21977，确认 checkpoint 路径**：
```bash
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Falcon3-7B-Base_d000_s42/lora_adapter/
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Falcon3-7B-Base_d100_s42/lora_adapter/
```

也检查 d005/d025/d050 checkpoint 是否在 cqa1 上：
```bash
ls -d /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Falcon3-7B-Base_d*/
```

2. **确认 GSM8K eval 脚本和参数**：
- 查看其他模型的 GSM8K eval 怎么做的：
```bash
grep -r "gsm8k" /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/ --include="*.py" --include="*.sh" -l
cat /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py --help 2>/dev/null || head -50 /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py
```
- 确认 GSM8K eval 用的参数（few-shot 数、格式、metric）与其他模型一致
- 搜索之前的 GSM8K eval 命令：
```bash
grep -r "gsm8k" /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/ --include="*.sh" -l
```

3. **确认 GSM8K 数据集可用**：
```bash
python -c "from datasets import load_dataset; ds = load_dataset('openai/gsm8k', 'main', split='test', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache'); print(f'GSM8K test: {len(ds)} items')"
```
如果离线模式失败，检查缓存是否已下载：
```bash
find /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache -name "*gsm8k*" -type d 2>/dev/null
```

4. **Dry-run 验证**：
构造完整的 eval 命令但不实际运行（或用 --help / --dry-run 模式）：
```bash
# 示例格式（需要确认实际参数）
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py \
    --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Falcon3-7B-Base \
    --adapter-path /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Falcon3-7B-Base_d000_s42/lora_adapter \
    --benchmark gsm8k \
    --output-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/Falcon3-7B-Base_d000_s42_gsm8k/ \
    --batch-size 4
```
如果 evaluate.py 支持 `--help`，先打印帮助确认参数。

5. **输出**：
   - 写入本地 `artifacts/falcon3_gsm8k_eval_commands.md`
   - 包含 d000 和 d100 的完整 eval 命令
   - 标注 S4 完成后直接复制粘贴即可启动
   - 估算运行时间（基于 GSM8K test set 大小和其他模型 eval 时长）

### 服务器
- cqa1-21977, root, conda base
- cuda:0 正在跑 S4 signals，不要启动 GPU 任务
- 只做 dry-run 和检查，不启动实际 eval

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/bin/bash\\n# Aligned-format MC eval for 7B: original + paraphrase\\n# Usage: CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 bash run_aligned_eval_7B.sh [original|paraphrase|both]\\nset -euo pipefail\\n\\nMODE=${1:-both}\\nshift 2>/dev/null || true\\n\\nsource /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null || true\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\\n\\nBASE=/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B\\nPARA_DIR=/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/paraphrases\\nBENCHMARKS=\\\"mmlu gsm8k arc_challenge humaneval\\\"\\nSCRIPT_DIR=$(cd \\\"$(dirname \\\"$0\\\")\\\" && pwd)\\nCKPT_BASE=/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints\\n\\nADAPTERS=(\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d000_s42\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d000_s123\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d005_s42\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d005_s123\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d025_s42\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d025_s123\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d050_s42\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d050_s123\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d100_s42\\n  Qwen2.5-7B_d100_s123\\n)\\n\\nfor adapter in \\\"${ADAPTERS[@]}\\\"; do\\n  ADAPTER_PATH=\\\"${CKPT_BASE}/${adapter}/lora_adapter\\\"\\n\\n  if [ ! -f \\\"${ADAPTER_PATH}/adapter_model.safetensors\\\" ]; then\\n    echo \\\"WARN: adapter not found: $ADAPTER_PATH, skipping\\\"\\n    continue\\n  fi\\n\\n  DOSAGE=$(echo \\\"$adapter\\\" | sed 's/Qwen2.5-7B_//')\\n\\n  if [ \\\"$MODE\\\" = \\\"original\\\" ] || [ \\\"$MODE\\\" = \\\"both\\\" ]; then\\n    OUT=/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/${adapter}_aligned\\n    if [ -f \\\"${OUT}/eval_results.json\\\" ]; then\\n      echo \\\"SKIP: ${OUT} already done\\\"\\n    else\\n      echo \\\"=== [original] ${adapter} -> ${OUT} ===\\\"\\n      python \\\"$SCRIPT_DIR/evaluate.py\\\" \\\\\\n        --model-path \\\"$BASE\\\" \\\\\\n        --adapter-path \\\"$ADAPTER_PATH\\\" \\\\\\n        --output-dir \\\"$OUT\\\" \\\\\\n        --benchmarks $BENCHMARKS \\\\\\n        --mc-format aligned \\\\\\n        --batch-size 2 \\\\\\n        \\\"$@\\\"\\n    fi\\n  fi\\n\\n  if [ \\\"$MODE\\\" = \\\"paraphrase\\\" ] || [ \\\"$MODE\\\" = \\\"both\\\" ]; then\\n    OUT_PARA=/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/${adapter}_para_aligned\\n    if [ -f \\\"${OUT_PARA}/eval_results.json\\\" ]; then\\n      echo \\\"SKIP: ${OUT_PARA} already done\\\"\\n    else\\n      echo \\\"=== [paraphrase] ${adapter} -> ${OUT_PARA} ===\\\"\\n      python \\\"$SCRIPT_DIR/evaluate.py\\\" \\\\\\n        --model-path \\\"$BASE\\\" \\\\\\n        --adapter-path \\\"$ADAPTER_PATH\\\" \\\\\\n        --paraphrase-dir \\\"$PARA_DIR\\\" \\\\\\n        --output-dir \\\"$OUT_PARA\\\" \\\\\\n        --benchmarks $BENCHMARKS \\\\\\n        --mc-format aligned \\\\\\n        --batch-size 2 \\\\\\n        \\\"$@\\\"\\n    fi\\n  fi\\ndone\\n\\necho \\\"All 7B aligned evals done.\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}
